Let $k$ be a field such that $k$ is a valuative relation, $k$ is a topological space and $k$ is a nonarchimedean local field. Let $X$ and $n$ be finite types. Let $m$ be a function from elements of $X$ to sets. Assume that $X$ is a linearly ordered set. Assume that for all elements $a_{3}$ of $X$, $m (a_{3})$ is a finite type. Assume that for all elements $a_{2}$ of $X$, $\mathrm{DecidableEq} (m (a_{2}))$. Let $W$ be an additive commutative group such that $W$ is a module over $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $e \in \EquivMACRoEB {\Sigma (\lambda a_{1} . m (a_{1}))}{n}$. Let $\rho$ be a representation of $\GlobalTheorySmGLLeviMACRoBNC {k}{m}$ and $W$. Let $f \in \GlobalTheorySmGLIndSpMACRoBME {e}{\rho}$. Let $p \in \SubtypeSet {x_{68}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {(\GlobalTheorySmGLparabMACRoBPP {k}{e})}{x_{68}})}$. def torusO (n : Type) : Subgroup (n → kˣ) where
  carrier := {t | ∀ i, valuation k (t i : k) = 1}
  one_mem' := by simp
  mul_mem' := by
    intro s t hs ht i
    simp [hs i, ht i]
  inv_mem' := by
    intro t ht i
    simp [ht i]
